### P8-6.2 · Order (browser seats serialized; every WebKit capture group its own `guarded --exclusive webkit --timeout 60` admission, witnesses `--light` outside the lock)

0. **The ruling sheet goes to the owner now** (P8-4). It is one message, and it is the only thing that can make pass 9 clean.
1. **H9.** H9-1..H9-5 and R-21's frozen list and printer (H9-6), each with its fixture PASS and planted FAIL in the self-test. Static only, `--light`.
2. **Floor v8.** Build R-20 on `floor-v7.patch`. Prove identity under R-22 (raw, else normalised plus the one Chromium dock bite). If it holds, run the static checks (DEL under H8 and H9, R-21's printed list read line by line, LAW, census, HOV, the ladder `diff`) and the toolchain with vitest (dist-demo built first), and no battery. Select any ruled owner option from the variants (`pass-4/fv3/q1c.*`, `fv3/q11b.*`), which then requires the full battery in both engines; build only what no variant carries. A Q-14 (b) ruling is built as R-2's deletion in the dark arm and re-reads row 12.
3. **The minor split v7.** Build R-23. Prove raw byte identity to the minor v6, then H7-3, H8-1 with H9 over its own patch, R-21's list, and the toolchain. No browser if the bytes hold.
4. **The one informational WebKit cell** (P8-5), queued after steps 2-3 in the floor v8 seat or its own seat, never in the critic. Not admitted in 90 min: owed, holds nothing.
5. **The critic**, one seat, adversarial, on floor v8 and the minor v7. It runs whether or not the owner has answered, because its job this pass includes confirming the fixed point; if unruled, it records that pass 9 cannot be clean. It checks R-20's four rewrites against the tree, the byte proofs, H9's plants, every line R-21's list prints, and R-23 against Crit §5.2's table. It may not add rows. A false sentence outside both of R-21's lists is reported for the next build and does not reopen rows 20 or FL-12 (R-21). A new defect class goes to the pass-10 instrument list.
6. **Agglomeration**, reading any owner rulings that have arrived.

### P8-6.3 · What would make pass 9 clean

Floor v8 is clean at **0 open rows of 44**:
- rows 20 and FL-12 close on R-20 under R-21: DEL with H9 at 0 over every touched file, floor v7 red under it, and every line of the frozen list read and true;

If the owner has not ruled by pass 9's critic, pass 9 cannot be clean, whatever the builds read.

Both ship from the minor split (v7 or later), never from the floor (P5-3.3), once G1-G7 are green (G6 per R-9, in W-0.1's text), Q-11's ruling is applied to G1's off-paper reading, R-23 is built, each gate is bound to one probe, and the ratchet is rebound at landing. 10.2.0 is also gated on D2 converging (O-88).

- **Floor v8 converges** once Q-1, Q-3, Q-11 (both clauses) and Q-14 are ruled and selected or built, R-20 closes rows 20 and FL-12 under R-21, and two consecutive passes are clean. It is then D3's converged specification: W-0.1's light arm and W-0.11's tokens through the minor split and its table (P5-3.3 as amended by P6-3.2, P7-3.2 and P8-3.2) in 10.2.0, and the rest in the W-H landing.

No route work can move those rows, so that is the loop's owner-gated fixed point. In that case **pass 10 is launched only when the owner's answers arrive**. - If the owner rules Q-3 (c), every banked family's re-trigger is re-read, and the next pass opens whichever fires.

### P9-6.2 · Order (browser seats serialized; every WebKit capture group its own `guarded --exclusive webkit --timeout 60` admission, witnesses `--light` outside the lock)

0. **The ruling sheet and R-21 go to the owner now** (P9-4). It is one message, and the owner's answers are the only thing that can make pass 10 clean.
1. **H10.** H10-1 and H10-2, each with its fixture PASS and planted FAIL in the self-test; the existing rulings files migrated to H10-2's `verdict` field in the same seat (the `REPORTED` `why` prefix becomes `verdict: reported`; no reading changes). Static only, `--light`.
2. **Floor v9.** Build R-24 on `floor-v8.patch`, in R-24's words. Prove raw identity to floor v8 in one session (R-22, R-27). If it holds, run the static checks (DEL under H8, H9 and H10; R-21's printed list, every line ruled under R-26; LAW, census, HOV, the ladder `diff`) and the toolchain with the dist-demo built before vitest, and no battery. Select any ruled owner option from the variants (`pass-4/fv3/q1c.*`, `fv3/q11b.*`), which then requires the full battery in both engines; build only what no variant carries. A Q-14 (b) ruling is built as R-2's deletion in the dark arm and re-reads row 12. Then queue the one informational WebKit cell (P9-5), its own admission, waiting up to 90 min.
3. **The minor split v8.** Build R-25. Prove raw identity to the minor v7, then H7-3, DEL with H9 and H10 over its own patch, R-21's list under R-26, and the toolchain. No browser if the bytes hold.
4. **The critic**, one seat, adversarial, on floor v9 and the minor v8, whether or not the owner has answered. It checks R-24's three rewrites word for word against the ruling, every printed line's ruling against every clause (R-26), the byte proofs, H10's plants, and R-25. It may not add rows. A false sentence outside both of R-21's lists is reported for the next build and does not reopen rows 20 or FL-12. A new defect class goes to the pass-11 instrument list. If unruled, it records that pass 10 cannot be clean and whether the fixed point is reached.
5. **Agglomeration**, reading any owner rulings that have arrived.

### P9-6.3 · What would make pass 10 clean

Floor v9 is clean at **0 open rows of 44**:
- rows 20 and FL-12 close on R-24 under R-21 and R-26: DEL with H9 and H10 at 0 over every touched file, floor v8 red under the re-ruled list, and every line of the frozen list ruled true on every clause;
- rows 9 and N-1 close when the owner rules Q-1, by selection under (a);
- rows 3 and N-2 close when the owner rules Q-11's chrome clause, by (a)'s text or by selecting `fv3/q11b.*`;
- row 23 closes when the owner rules Q-11's content clause, Q-3 and Q-14 and accepts the frames.

If the owner has not ruled by pass 10's critic, pass 10 cannot be clean, whatever the builds read.

**W-0.1's and W-0.11's entry gate after pass 9:** 0 of 2 clean passes. Earliest landing: the close of **pass 11**, if passes 10 and 11 are both clean. Both ship from the minor split (v8 or later), never from the floor (P5-3.3), once G1-G7 are green (G6 per R-9, in W-0.1's text), Q-11's ruling is applied to G1's off-paper reading, R-25 is built, each gate is bound to one probe, and the ratchet is rebound at landing. 10.2.0 is also gated on D2 converging (O-88).

### P9-6.4 · What would end the loop

- **Floor v9 converges** once Q-1, Q-3, Q-11 (both clauses) and Q-14 are ruled and selected or built, rows 20 and FL-12 close under R-21 and R-26, and two consecutive passes are clean. It is then D3's converged specification: W-0.1's light arm and W-0.11's tokens through the minor split and its table (P5-3.3 as amended by P6-3.2, P7-3.2, P8-3.2 and P9-3.2) in 10.2.0, and the rest in the W-H landing.
- **The fixed point.** If pass 10 closes rows 20 and FL-12 and the sheet is still unanswered, floor v9 reads 39/44 with only rows 3, 9, 23, N-1 and N-2 open. No route work can move those rows, so that is the loop's owner-gated fixed point. **Pass 11 is then launched only when the owner's answers arrive.** A further pass without them re-measures the same tree and cannot be clean, and the record will say so plainly.
- **If pass 10's critic still finds rows 20 and FL-12 open** on a printed line, the next build is again comment only, in words the agglomeration fixes, and the R-21 line beside the sheet becomes the owner's to settle before any pass after it.
- **C″ stays banked** unless a re-trigger fires. The live one is Q-11's content clause ruled "not accepted" (re-trigger (c)). It would re-enter on its 48 rows, with C-4 and the chrome held floor owed first.
- If the owner rules Q-3 (c), every banked family's re-trigger is re-read, and the next pass opens whichever fires.
- If the owner overrides R-14, row 6 returns to FV5 §5's options, and the registry rules among them. If the owner overrides R-21, rows 20 and FL-12 return to P7-6.3's open-ended reading, and the fixed point cannot be promised.

## P10-6 · Pass-11 plan

### P10-6.1 · When pass 11 launches

**Only when the owner answers R-21** (P10-4). Pass 10 was the last pass that launches without the owner (P9-0). A pass 11 run without that answer would build R-28 under R-21 (a), and four passes show a fresh critic then finds the next line; it cannot promise an end and cannot be clean without the sheet. The record says so plainly: **D3 is parked on the owner from the close of pass 10.** The other answers may come in the same message; any that do not take their defaults.

### P10-6.2 · Routes

| route | centre | judged on |
|---|---|---|
| **floor v10** = floor v9 + R-28 (+ whatever the owner has ruled, selected from `pass-4/fv3/q1c.*`, `fv3/q11b.*`) | the bound, static ink, FL-1..FL-16 | **the same 44 rows** (24 core + FL-1..FL-16 + N-1..N-4). No row is added, struck or renamed. Rows 20 and FL-12 are read under R-21 as the owner settles it, R-26 and R-30 |
| **the minor split v9** (a build, not a route) | minor v8 + R-29 | raw identity to the minor v8; H7-3; DEL with H9-H11 over its own patch at 0; R-21 (b) under R-26 at 0 unread; the toolchain |
| C″ | BANK | not run unless a re-trigger fires (Q-11 content "not accepted" fires (c)) |
| D3-G | BLOCK | not run unless Q-13 (b) or an engine ships the primitive |

### P10-6.3 · Order (no browser unless an owner variant is selected; then every WebKit capture group is its own `guarded --exclusive webkit --timeout 60` admission, witnesses `--light` outside the lock, a cell not admitted in 90 min owed and holding nothing)

0. **The sheet, R-21 first, goes to the owner now** (P10-4).
1. **H11.** H11-1 and H11-2, each with its fixture and plant in the self-test. Static only, `--light`.
2. **Floor v10.** Build R-28 on `floor-v9.patch`, in R-28's words. Prove raw identity to floor v9 in one session (R-22, R-27). Run the static checks (DEL under H8-H11; R-21's printed list under `crit-fv10-rulings-proposed.json`, every line re-read under R-26 and R-30 with H11-2's print; LAW, census, HOV, the ladder `diff`) and the toolchain, dist-demo before vitest. No battery. Select a ruled owner variant if one is ruled; that requires the full battery in both engines. A Q-14 (b) ruling is built as R-2's deletion in the dark arm and re-reads row 12.
3. **The minor split v9.** Build R-29. Raw identity to the minor v8, H7-3, DEL, R-21's list under `MS8-rulings-proposed.json`, the toolchain. No browser.
4. **The critic**, one seat, adversarial, on floor v10 and the minor v9. It checks R-28's and R-29's words against the rulings, every printed line under R-26 and R-30, the byte proofs and H11's plants. It may not add rows. Under R-21 (b) a false printed line it finds is proposed as a measured comment delta and goes to the next comment-only build or the landing docs pass; under (a) it reopens rows 20 and FL-12.
5. **Agglomeration**, reading the owner's answers.

### P10-6.4 · What would make pass 11 clean

Floor v10 is clean at **0 open rows of 44**:
- rows 20 and FL-12 close on R-28 under R-21 as the owner settles it, R-26 and R-30: DEL at 0, the list PASS 207 (floor v9 red at the seven lines), and, under (a) or (c), no further false printed line found by the critic;
- rows 9 and N-1 close when the owner rules Q-1, by selection under (a);
- rows 3 and N-2 close when the owner rules Q-11's chrome clause, by (a)'s text or by selecting `fv3/q11b.*`;
- row 23 closes when the owner rules Q-11's content clause, Q-3 and Q-14 and accepts the frames.

**W-0.1's and W-0.11's entry gate after pass 10:** 0 of 2 clean passes. Earliest landing: the close of **pass 12**, if passes 11 and 12 are both clean. Both ship from the minor split (v9 or later), never from the floor (P5-3.3), once G1-G7 are green (G6 per R-9, in W-0.1's text), Q-11's ruling is applied to G1's off-paper reading, R-29 is built, the landing docs pass is done, each gate is bound to one probe, and the ratchet is rebound. 10.2.0 is also gated on D2 converging (O-88).

### P10-6.5 · What would end the loop

- **Floor v10 converges** once R-21 is settled, Q-1, Q-3, Q-11 (both clauses) and Q-14 are ruled and selected or built, rows 20 and FL-12 close, and two consecutive passes are clean. It is then D3's converged specification: W-0.1's light arm and W-0.11's tokens through the minor split and its table (P5-3.3 as amended through P10-3.2) in 10.2.0, and the rest in the W-H landing.
- **The fixed point.** If the owner settles R-21 but not the sheet, and pass 11 closes rows 20 and FL-12, floor v10 reads 39/44 with only rows 3, 9, 23, N-1 and N-2 open. That is the owner-gated fixed point, and pass 12 launches only when the sheet's answers arrive.
- **C″ stays banked** unless a re-trigger fires; the live one is Q-11's content clause ruled "not accepted" (c), re-entering on its 48 rows with C-4 and the chrome held floor owed first.
- If the owner rules Q-3 (c), every banked family's re-trigger is re-read, and the next pass opens whichever fires.
- If the owner overrides R-14, row 6 returns to FV5 §5's options. If the owner chooses R-21 (c), rows 20 and FL-12 return to P7-6.3's open-ended reading, and the fixed point cannot be promised.
